# GEOSHAPE queries and validity against RediSearch 8.10.1's Boost.Geometry, recorded from the random
# differential test (`redis-engine` `geometry::difftest`): polygons on shared grids, with holes, around
# the poles and across the antimeridian, and points on their vertices and edges, each run with
# CONTAINS / WITHIN / INTERSECTS / DISJOINT. These pin down `relate`'s areal/areal analysis for WITHIN
# (identical polygons, shared edges), `disjoint` (any turn, else the exterior ring's first point covered),
# the R-tree's node predicates (internal boxes collapsed at a pole), segment envelopes expanded without
# normalizing, and the build's contracted multiply-adds (side tests, the cartesian area's vectorized sum).
